# Playtest: mixed_resist_then_book
seed=110
interpreter=OllamaInterpreter

## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> wait

In the dim, unyielding expanse of your cell, you find yourself still, the silence as relentless as the stone walls encasing you. The bed, cup, book, and door remain stationary, unresponsive to your presence. Your skin, unaccustomed to the grime, feels raw and unclean - a stark contrast to the thin words that struggle to escape your lips. Yet, you wait, as the cell remains stubbornly quiet, offering no respite or solace. The wait continues, the cell's tranquility undisturbed.

## Turn 3
> wait

The observation slit in the door creaks open, revealing a figure beyond, their motions and repeated sounds urging you to step back from the door. The cell remains silent save for the occasional echo of their plea. Your feet remain planted, hands clenched around the book you hold, as the words you speak seem to thin in the stillness. ## Turn 4
> I will not back away

In the dim confines of your cell, the staff member's gaze shifts, momentarily pausing the repetitive gesture. The air thickens with anticipation, a palpable tension that hangs heavy between you and the figure beyond the door. The cell remains silent, save for the occasional echo of your breath and the distant clatter of unknown activities.

You stand firm, your feet planted unyieldingly on the cold, stone floor. The staff member, sensing your resolve, turns, seemingly to call for assistance. The slit in the door, once a source of disquiet, now serves as a testament to your unwavering stance. The staff member's gesture resumes, but the urgency seems diminished, a subtle shift in the power dynamic that permeates the cell. The cell breathes with you, a shared silence that echoes your defiance. The staff member's calls for you to step back continue, but the door remains unyielding, mirroring your own resolve.

In the confined cell, the slit in the door, your only window to the outside, remains open. The figure beyond, a tall, silent presence, continues their persistent gesturing and short, unintelligible sounds. The demand is clear: step away from the door.

You try to speak, but your words come out thinner than intended, a result of language compromise. Your utterance, "Refuse?", hangs in the air, understood by the figure who repeats a short sound and a gesture, insisting once more: back. They are still waiting, their gaze unyielding.

The cell stays quiet, the air heavy with unspoken tension. Your hands, bound or not, limit your movements, but you hold your ground, the unclean feel of your skin a constant reminder of your current state. The figure outside waits, their patience worn thin, yet they do not force the issue, for now. The cell, your temporary prison, remains your sanctuary.

## Turn 8
> read the book

In the cramped confines of your cell, the silence thickens as the staff member, a towering figure of practiced efficiency, waits with unyielding patience. The book, a potential source of solace, remains untouched, your hands bound and the institution leaving no quiet for even a whispered page turn. The door, a barrier between you and them, looms as a constant reminder of the rules that govern this place. The staff member's gaze, steady and unwavering, communicates the expectation for you to step away from the door, a demand that your efforts to resist have so far proven futile. The book, left closed and unread, is a testament to the institution's unyielding grip.

## Turn 9
> look around

In the corridor, the institution's rhythm permeates, as the same bolts adorn the door that just opened, guiding you out of the cell. Your arms are held by hands, a constant reminder of your confinement. The corridor, plain and unyielding, mirrors the silence that surrounds you. A bed lies beneath you, a cup sits beside it, and a book rests on the floor, face-down. The door on this side has no handle, a symbol of your lack of control. Above, a narrow slit sits at eye level, offering a glimpse of the world outside. Your mouth tastes stale, a lingering reminder of your captivity.

Dom, your only known companion, is still present. The orderly who seemed younger and tense, rule-bound, is waiting, insisting you stay away from the door.

A figure approaches, larger and more imposing than the first. Their arrival signifies that they are ready to enter, regardless of your movement. The corridor echoes with their foot

## Turn 10
> continue

The corridor stretches out before you, identical to the one that confined you earlier. Your arms are once again grasped, this time by two different figures – Dom and Ben, as if the institution were a single, unyielding entity. The large, silent one, Dom, maintains a firm grip, his movements purposeful and steady. The younger, anxious one, Ben, seems on edge, his grip tightening every so often, as if he's bracing for something. The air around you feels heavy with unspoken rules and unyielding routine. The scent of unwashed bodies and sterilized surfaces fills your nose, a constant reminder of your new reality. Your words seem to get lost in the silence, leaving you with a sense of isolation and vulnerability. Despite your desire to understand your surroundings, the questions you ask only seem to echo off the cold, unfeeling walls.

## Turn 13
> stay awake

In the washroom, the staff members wait, their gazes fixed on you. The large, silent one seems patient, while the younger, tense one fidgets with the basin. The water in the basin remains still, the soap smelling sharp against the sterile air.

Your eyes burn from the fight against sleep, the bed in your mind a distant memory. Your hands clench, feeling the bindings that limit your movement. The basin and water remain, a physical reminder of what they expect.

"They are still waiting: wash," one of them says, their voice echoing off the cold, hard walls. The words come out thinner than you intend, a testament to the tension in the air.

You resist the urge to slip back into sleep, your skin feeling unclean enough to notice. The fear keeps your shoulders tight, but you remain awake, your mind focused on the task at hand. The basin and water still wait.

## Turn 14
> fight sleep

In the dimly lit washroom, the air heavy with medicinal soap, the larger, silent staff member watches you with a practiced gaze. The younger, tense one stands beside them, their hands clenched tightly. The basin waits, water steaming slightly.

Your eyes burn from fighting sleep, a dull tiredness lingering behind them. Your skin, unclean from your journey, feels noticeably so. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, and words seem to come out thinner than you intend. Despite this, you resist the call of sleep, hands clenched firmly.

Suddenly, the younger staff member speaks, their voice echoing off the cold walls, "They are still waiting: wash." The tension in the room ratchets up a notch, the silence heavy with unspoken rules. The basin and water still wait, the routine uninterrupted.

## Turn 15
> read the book

In the mess room, a table stands before you, a bowl of something warm resting atop, steam rising. A spoon lies beside it. The air is filled with the scent of food, a stark contrast to the sterile washroom you left behind. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, are waiting expectantly, their gazes fixed on you. Iven, the gentle believer, is nearby, his expression calm.

Despite your resistance, you are compelled to undergo a forced washing, leaving you with a sense of vulnerability and an unmistakable cleanliness. The air now smells less like you, and your skin feels different under your touch.

The staff members, however, remain unfazed. They are still waiting, their focus now on the bowl in front of you. The spoon in your hand trembles slightly, a testament to your fear and the tension that permeates the room. The staff members continue to expect you to eat, or at least leave the food untouched

## Turn 16
> stop reading

In the mess, the air heavy with anticipation, the staff members' eyes never straying from the bowl before you. The heat rising from the meal, the spoon laid beside it, a stark contrast to the coldness of the room. Yet, despite the silence and the tension, you find yourself not reading, your book unopened. A dull tiredness sits behind your eyes, fear keeping your shoulders tight, your hands or bindings limiting what you can move. Words come out thinner than you intend. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, continue to wait, their gaze fixed on the uneaten meal. The gentle believer, Iven, watches with a gentle frown, the unresolved tension still hanging in the air. The staff's repeated plea echoes in your mind: "They are still waiting: eat."

## Turn 18
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you're returned to the same space where whatever you moved remains untouched. The air is heavy with the weight of fatigue. The bed beneath you offers a worn comfort, the cup beside it a silent promise of respite. A book lies face-down on the floor, its pages waiting to be read. The door, devoid of a handle on this side, remains shut, its narrow slit casting a faint light on the room. The taste of stale air fills your mouth, your skin feeling worse for wear.

You wait, your limbs heavy, staying upright a struggle. Fear keeps your shoulders tight, and words come out thinner than intended. The hours tick by, the institution's expectations of rest hanging heavy in the air. The silence is broken only by the distant sounds of the facility.

Eventually, the food arrives, easing the gnawing hunger in your stomach a little. The day is winding toward sleep, and you find yourself growing more tired with each passing

## Turn 21
> wait

In the dim light of the cell, a new day breaks, echoing the institution's formal announcement. The cold stone walls remain mute, a stark contrast to the anticipation that fills the space. You wait, your body tense, the fear palpable in the air. Time seems to stretch as the cell maintains its eerie silence. Despite your efforts to stay awake, your body succumbs, waking later with your face pressed against the hard, unyielding wall. The day unfolds, a new chapter in this ongoing saga.

## Turn 22
> wait

The morning collection is more formal. You're led from the cell into a plain corridor, the same bolts on the door echoing the one from your cell. Hands grip your arms, pulling you along, the institution's pace steady and unyielding. The cell remains quiet, your wait continuing. The echoes of footsteps are the only sounds, a reminder of your journey. The air is heavy with anticipation.

Fear keeps your shoulders tight, and words come out thinner than intended. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, escort you towards the interview room. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic, is also present, her presence a constant reminder of the challenges ahead. The unresolved tension is palpable, the expectation of compliance with the escort heavy in the air. The cell remains quiet, a stark contrast to the corridor's movement. The morning continues, and with it, the formal retrieval process.

## Turn 24
> wait

In the brightly lit, sterile interview room, you find yourself facing Veyra, a figure clad in a precisely fitted collar, their demeanor a blend of professional kindness and unyielding control. Iven, a gentle presence, is also present. The room is adorned with a table and a selection of images and objects, reminiscent of a lesson, though the subject remains unclear. The air is thick with unresolved tension, as the staff members await your answers to their questions. Your heart races, fear keeping your shoulders tight, and words come out thinner than you intend. The room, once the corridor, has transformed into a stage for this interrogation. The interview has commenced.

## Turn 25
> wait

In the facility moment, the situation shifts as you find yourself amidst three figures: an older one, a younger one, and a smaller figure, possibly a brother. The air carries a distinct scent that stirs something within you. A name, unspoken, conjures an image. Memories, some vivid, some disconcerting, flood your consciousness without invitation.

Fear tightens your shoulders, and words slip out thinner than you intend. You wait, your feet planted firmly on the ground, hands still, as if trying to hold back the tide of memories. The staff members, one controlled and kind, the other large and practical, wait for your answer. Iven, gentle and believing, also remains silent, awaiting the end of the contract. The unresolved tension in the room thickens as the silence grows.

> wait

The large staff member watches you, their gaze unyielding.
